## `AFG-7912-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Travel-Liability-Cover Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Inbound Travel DMC Qualification & Tourist Coach Safety Compliance
- **Intro:** Travel-Liability-Cover Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Inbound Travel DMC Qualification & Tourist Coach Safety Compliance — Pre-Calibration Safety Lockout/Tagout (LOTO) Verification: Voltage at bench input must be 0 VAC/VDC. Engine start attempt must produce no cranking.
- **Key tolerance:** Voltage at bench input must be 0 VAC/VDC. Engine start attempt must produce no cranking.
- **Standards:** `ISO 9001:2015`, `ISO/IEC 17025:2017`, `ISO 39001:2012`, `Afghan National Standards Authority (ANSA) - Motor Vehicle Safet`
